        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;

$curl = curl_init();

curl_setopt_array($curl, [
  CURLOPT_URL =&gt; "https://api.tavily.com/extract",
  CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER =&gt; true,
  CURLOPT_ENCODING =&gt; "",
  CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS =&gt; 10,
  CURLOPT_TIMEOUT =&gt; 30,
  CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION =&gt; CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,
  CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST =&gt; "POST",
  CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS =&gt; json_encode([
    'urls' =&gt; 'https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence',
    'query' =&gt; '&lt;string&gt;',
    'chunks_per_source' =&gt; 3,
    'extract_depth' =&gt; 'basic',
    'include_images' =&gt; false,
    'include_favicon' =&gt; false,
    'format' =&gt; 'markdown',
    'timeout' =&gt; 'None',
    'include_usage' =&gt; false
  ]),
  CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER =&gt; [
    "Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;",
    "Content-Type: application/json"
  ],
]);

$response = curl_exec($curl);
$err = curl_error($curl);

curl_close($curl);

if ($err) {
  echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
} else {
  echo $response;
}</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#FFFFFF;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-hidden="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" hidden id="base-ui-_R_1cqbddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_" tabindex="0" inert="" data-index="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative focus-visible:outline-hidden before:content-[''] before:absolute before:inset-0 before:z-10 before:pointer-events-none before:rounded-xt focus-visible:before:outline focus-visible:before:outline-1 focus-visible:before:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:before:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:before:outline-primary-light data-hidden:hidden [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:rounded-xt!"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:100%;background-color:#FFFFFF;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-white"><div role="presentation" data-id="base-ui-_R_7cqbddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre flex-none h-full text-xs leading-[1.35rem]" data-component-part="code-group-tab-content"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes"><code>package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"net/http"
	"io"
)

func main() {

	url := "https://api.tavily.com/extract"

	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"urls\": \"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence\",\n  \"query\": \"&lt;string&gt;\",\n  \"chunks_per_source\": 3,\n  \"extract_depth\": \"basic\",\n  \"include_images\": false,\n  \"include_favicon\": false,\n  \"format\": \"markdown\",\n  \"timeout\": \"None\",\n  \"include_usage\": false\n}")

	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)

	req.Header.Add("Authorization", "Bearer &lt;token&gt;")
	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")

	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)

	defer res.Body.Close()
	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)

	fmt.Println(string(body))

}</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#FFFFFF;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-hidden="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" hidden id="base-ui-_R_1kqbddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_" tabindex="0" inert="" data-index="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative focus-visible:outline-hidden before:content-[''] before:absolute before:inset-0 before:z-10 before:pointer-events-none before:rounded-xt focus-visible:before:outline focus-visible:before:outline-1 focus-visible:before:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:before:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:before:outline-primary-light data-hidden:hidden [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:rounded-xt!"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:100%;background-color:#FFFFFF;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-white"><div role="presentation" data-id="base-ui-_R_7kqbddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre flex-none h-full text-xs leading-[1.35rem]" data-component-part="code-group-tab-content"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes"><code>HttpResponse&lt;String&gt; response = Unirest.post("https://api.tavily.com/extract")

$curl = curl_init();

curl_setopt_array($curl, [
  CURLOPT_URL =&gt; "https://api.tavily.com/extract",
  CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER =&gt; true,
  CURLOPT_ENCODING =&gt; "",
  CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS =&gt; 10,
  CURLOPT_TIMEOUT =&gt; 30,
  CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION =&gt; CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,
  CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST =&gt; "POST",
  CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS =&gt; json_encode([
    'urls' =&gt; 'https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence',
    'query' =&gt; '&lt;string&gt;',
    'chunks_per_source' =&gt; 3,
    'extract_depth' =&gt; 'basic',
    'include_images' =&gt; false,
    'include_favicon' =&gt; false,
    'format' =&gt; 'markdown',
    'timeout' =&gt; 'None',
    'include_usage' =&gt; false
  ]),
  CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER =&gt; [
    "Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;",
    "Content-Type: application/json"
  ],
]);

$response = curl_exec($curl);
$err = curl_error($curl);

curl_close($curl);

if ($err) {
  echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
} else {
  echo $response;

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"net/http"
	"io"
)

func main() {

	url := "https://api.tavily.com/extract"

	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"urls\": \"https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Artificial_intelligence\",\n  \"query\": \"&lt;string&gt;\",\n  \"chunks_per_source\": 3,\n  \"extract_depth\": \"basic\",\n  \"include_images\": false,\n  \"include_favicon\": false,\n  \"format\": \"markdown\",\n  \"timeout\": \"None\",\n  \"include_usage\": false\n}")

	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)

	req.Header.Add("Authorization", "Bearer &lt;token&gt;")
	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")

	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)

	defer res.Body.Close()
	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)

	fmt.Println(string(body))

}</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#FFFFFF;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-hidden="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" hidden id="base-ui-_R_d6b4jddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_" tabindex="0" inert="" data-index="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative focus-visible:outline-hidden before:content-[''] before:absolute before:inset-0 before:z-10 before:pointer-events-none before:rounded-xt focus-visible:before:outline focus-visible:before:outline-1 focus-visible:before:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:before:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:before:outline-primary-light data-hidden:hidden [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:rounded-xt!"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:100%;background-color:#FFFFFF;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-white"><div role="presentation" data-id="base-ui-_R_1t6b4jddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full!
